ensures the numeric expressions of
(\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}},\hat{\textup{se}}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}})
in Theorem
S2
(
i
).
We verify below Theorem
S2
(
ii
).

Proof of Theorem
S2
(
ii
).

Let
\hat{\beta}_{X,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}
denote the estimated coefficient vector of
X_{i}
from
\texttt{2sls}(Y_{i}\sim D_{i}+C_{i}+X_{i}\mid Z_{i}+C_{i}+X_{i})
. Applying Lemma
S10
with
D_{i}^{*}=(D_{i},X_{i})
,
Z_{i}^{*}=(Z_{i},X_{i})
, and
W_{i}=C_{i}
ensures that for the purpose of Theorem
S2
, the covariate-adjusted
2sfe
is numerically equivalent to its
C
-partialled out variant,

\displaystyle\texttt{2sls}(\dot{Y}_{i}\sim\dot{D}_{i}+\dot{X}_{i}\mid\dot{Z}_{i}+\dot{X}_{i}),

(S112)

where the estimated coefficients of
\dot{D}_{i}
and
\dot{X}_{i}
coincide with
\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}
and
\hat{\beta}_{X,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}
, and the cluster-robust standard error and IV residuals coincide with
\hat{\textup{se}}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}
and
r_{i,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}
, respectively.
Given that (
S112
) is a just-identified
2sls
, the estimation equation implies

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}\\
\hat{\beta}_{X,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\begin{pmatrix}\dot{Z}_{i}\\
\dot{X}_{i}\end{pmatrix}(\dot{D}_{i},\dot{X}^{\top}_{i})\right\}^{-1}\left\{N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\begin{pmatrix}\dot{Z}_{i}\\
\dot{X}_{i}\end{pmatrix}\dot{Y}_{i}\right\}

(S113)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}S_{ZD,\textup{in}}&S_{XZ,\textup{in}}^{\top}\\
S_{XD,\textup{in}}&S_{X,\textup{in}}\end{pmatrix}^{-1}\left\{N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\begin{pmatrix}\dot{Z}_{i}\\
\dot{X}_{i}\end{pmatrix}\dot{Y}_{i}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{N}^{-1}\left\{N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\begin{pmatrix}\dot{Z}_{i}\\
\dot{X}_{i}\end{pmatrix}\dot{Y}_{i}\right\},

where

\begin{array}[]{l}\Phi_{N}=\begin{pmatrix}S_{ZD,\textup{in}}&S_{XZ,\textup{in}}^{\top}\\
S_{XD,\textup{in}}&S_{X,\textup{in}}\end{pmatrix},\\
S_{XZ,\textup{in}}=\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\dot{X}_{i}\dot{Z}_{i},\quad S_{X,\textup{in}}=\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\dot{X}_{i}\dot{X}^{\top}_{i},\quad S_{XD,\textup{in}}=\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\dot{X}_{i}\dot{D}_{i}.\end{array}

Recall from (
S20
) that
R_{i,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}=\dot{A}_{i}-\dot{X}^{\top}_{i}\gamma_{X,A}
, where
\gamma_{X,A}=\{\E(S_{X,\textup{in}})\}^{-1}\E(S_{XA,\textup{in}})
.
Recall from (
S30
) the definition of
\Omega_{N,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}
.
Let

B_{i}=\begin{pmatrix}\dot{Z}_{i}\\
\dot{X}_{i}\end{pmatrix}R_{i,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}},\quad\bar{B}=\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}B_{i},

(S114)

with

\Omega_{N,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}\overset{\eqref{eq:omgnlsx_omgnfex_def}}{=}N^{-1}\cov\left\{\displaystyle\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\begin{pmatrix}\dot{Z}_{i}\\
\dot{X}_{i}\end{pmatrix}R_{i,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}\right\}=\cov(\sqrt{N}\bar{B}).

(S115)

Equation (
S113
) ensures

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}-\tau_{\textup{c}}\\
\hat{\beta}_{X,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}-\gamma_{X,A}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:fex_ee_1}}{=}

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}\\
\hat{\beta}_{X,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}\end{pmatrix}-\begin{pmatrix}\tau_{\textup{c}}\\
\gamma_{X,A}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{N}^{-1}\left\{N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\begin{pmatrix}\dot{Z}_{i}\\
\dot{X}_{i}\end{pmatrix}\dot{Y}_{i}-N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\begin{pmatrix}\dot{Z}_{i}\\
\dot{X}_{i}\end{pmatrix}(\dot{D}_{i},\dot{X}^{\top}_{i})\begin{pmatrix}\tau_{\textup{c}}\\
\gamma_{X,A}\end{pmatrix}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{N}^{-1}\left[N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\begin{pmatrix}\dot{Z}_{i}\\
\dot{X}_{i}\end{pmatrix}\left\{\dot{Y}_{i}-(\dot{D}_{i},\dot{X}^{\top}_{i})\begin{pmatrix}\tau_{\textup{c}}\\
\gamma_{X,A}\end{pmatrix}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:rrifex_def}}{=}

\displaystyle\Phi_{N}^{-1}\left\{N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\begin{pmatrix}\dot{Z}_{i}\\
\dot{X}_{i}\end{pmatrix}R_{i,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:bi_fex}}{=}

\displaystyle\Phi_{N}^{-1}\bar{B}

so that

\displaystyle\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}-\tau_{\textup{c}}=(1,0_{p}^{\top})\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}-\tau_{\textup{c}}\\
\hat{\beta}_{X,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}-\gamma_{X,A}\end{pmatrix}=(1,0_{p}^{\top})\Phi_{N}^{-1}\bar{B}=\Gamma_{N}\bar{B},

(S116)

where

\Gamma_{N}=(1,0_{p}^{\top})\Phi_{N}^{-1}\in\mathbb{R}^{1\times(1+p)}.

We verify in Sections
S4.2.1
–
S4.2.2

(i)

V_{{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}},N}^{-1/2}\cdot\sqrt{N}(\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}-\tau_{\textup{c}})\rightsquigarrow\mathcal{N}(0,1)
;

(ii)

N\hat{\textup{se}}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}^{2}/V_{{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}},N}=1+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
,

respectively, which together imply
(\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}-\tau_{\textup{c}})/\hat{\textup{se}}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}\rightsquigarrow\mathcal{N}(0,1)
.

S4.2.1
Proof of
V_{{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}},N}^{-1/2}\cdot\sqrt{N}(\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}-\tau_{\textup{c}})\rightsquigarrow\mathcal{N}(0,1)
.

Recall from Lemma
S14
that
\kappa_{N}>0
under Assumption
S2
(
iv
).
Let

\begin{array}[]{l}\Gamma^{*}_{N}=\dfrac{1}{\kappa_{N}\cdot\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}}}(1,0_{p}^{\top}),\\
B_{i}^{*}=\Gamma^{*}_{N}B_{i}\overset{\eqref{eq:bi_fex}}{=}\dfrac{1}{\kappa_{N}\cdot\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}}}\dot{Z}_{i}R_{i,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}},\quad\bar{B}^{*}=\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}B_{i}^{*}=\Gamma^{*}_{N}\bar{B},\end{array}

(S117)

with

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\var(\sqrt{N}\bar{B}^{*})&=&\var\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}B_{i}^{*}\right)\\
&\overset{\textup{Assumption~\ref{assm:cs}}}{=}&N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\var\left(\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}B_{i}^{*}\right)\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{(\kappa_{N}\cdot\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}})^{2}}\cdot N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\var\left(\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}\dot{Z}_{i}R_{i,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}\right)\\
&=&V_{{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}},N},\\
\Gamma^{*}_{N}\Omega_{N,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}\Gamma_{N}^{*\top}&\overset{\eqref{eq:omgnfex}}{=}&\Gamma^{*}_{N}\cov(\sqrt{N}\bar{B})\Gamma_{N}^{*\top}\\
&=&\var(\sqrt{N}\Gamma^{*}_{N}\bar{B})=\var(\sqrt{N}\bar{B}^{*})=V_{{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}},N}.\end{array}

(S118)

Recall that
\Gamma_{N}=(1,0_{p}^{\top})\Phi_{N}^{-1}
from (
S116
).
We establish below

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}\Gamma_{N}=\Gamma^{*}_{N}+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1),\\
\Omega_{N,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}^{-1/2}\cdot\sqrt{N}\bar{B}\;\rightsquigarrow\;\mathcal{N}(0_{1+p},I_{1+p}),\quad V_{{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}},N}^{-1/2}\cdot\sqrt{N}\bar{B}^{*}\;\rightsquigarrow\;\mathcal{N}(0,1).\end{array}

and then use (
S4.2.1
) to establish the central limit theorem for
\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}-\tau_{\textup{c}}=\Gamma_{N}\bar{B}
in (
S116
).

S4.2.1.1
Proof of
\Gamma_{N}=\Gamma^{*}_{N}+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
in (
S4.2.1
).

From (
S113
), Lemma
S14
(
iii
) implies

\displaystyle\Phi_{N}\overset{\textup{\eqref{eq:fex_ee_1}}}{=}\begin{pmatrix}S_{ZD,\textup{in}}&S_{XZ,\textup{in}}^{\top}\\
S_{XD,\textup{in}}&S_{X,\textup{in}}\end{pmatrix}\overset{\textup{Lemma~\ref{lem:fex}}}{=}\E(\Phi_{N})+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

(S122)

Under Assumptions
2
–
3
,
Lemma
S12
ensures
\E(S_{ZD,\textup{in}})=\kappa_{N}\cdot\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}}
and
\E(S_{XZ,\textup{in}})=0
, so that

\displaystyle\E(\Phi_{N})\overset{\textup{\eqref{eq:fex_ee_1}}}{=}\begin{pmatrix}\E(S_{ZD,\textup{in}})&\E(S_{XZ,\textup{in}}^{\top})\\
\E(S_{XD,\textup{in}})&\E(S_{X,\textup{in}})\end{pmatrix}\overset{\textup{Lemma~\ref{lem:cef}}}{=}\begin{pmatrix}\kappa_{N}\cdot\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}}&0_{p}^{\top}\\
\E(S_{XD,\textup{in}})&\E(S_{X,\textup{in}})\end{pmatrix}.

(S123)

Assumption
S2
(
iv
) ensures
\E(S_{X,\textup{in}})
is full rank and
\kappa_{N}>0
by Lemma
S14
, so that
\{\E(\Phi_{N})\}^{-1}
is well defined,
with

\Gamma_{N}\overset{\eqref{eq:dhtfex}}{=}(1,0_{p}^{\top})\Phi_{N}^{-1}=(1,0_{p}^{\top})\cdot\{\E(\Phi_{N})\}^{-1}+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

(S124)

From (
S123
), the formula for block matrix inverse in Lemma
S3
ensures

\displaystyle\{\E(\Phi_{N})\}^{-1}\overset{\textup{\eqref{eq:ephin}+Lemma~\ref{lem:block_mat}}}{=}\begin{pmatrix}\{\E(S_{ZD,\textup{in}})\}^{-1}&0_{p}^{\top}\\
-\{\E(S_{X,\textup{in}})\}^{-1}\E(S_{XD,\textup{in}})\{\E(S_{ZD,\textup{in}})\}^{-1}&\{\E(S_{X,\textup{in}})\}^{-1}\end{pmatrix},

so that

\displaystyle(1,0_{p}^{\top})\cdot\{\E(\Phi_{N})\}^{-1}=\left(\dfrac{1}{\E(S_{ZD,\textup{in}})},0_{p}^{\top}\right)=\dfrac{1}{\kappa_{N}\cdot\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}}}(1,0_{p}^{\top})\overset{\eqref{eq:bis_fex}}{=}\Gamma^{*}_{N}.

Plugging this in (
S124
) verifies
\Gamma_{N}-\Gamma^{*}_{N}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
in (
S4.2.1
).

S4.2.1.2
Proof of
\Omega_{N,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}^{-1/2}\cdot\sqrt{N}\bar{B}\rightsquigarrow\mathcal{N}(0_{1+p},I_{1+p})
in (

).

The definition of
B_{i}
in (
S114
) ensures that

\displaystyle B_{i}=\begin{pmatrix}\dot{Z}_{i}\\
\dot{X}_{i}\end{pmatrix}R_{i,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}=\begin{pmatrix}\dot{Z}_{i}\\
\dot{X}_{i}\end{pmatrix}(\dot{A}_{i}-\dot{X}^{\top}_{i}\gamma_{X,A})=\begin{pmatrix}\dot{Z}_{i}\dot{A}_{i}-\dot{Z}_{i}\dot{X}^{\top}_{i}\gamma_{X,A}\\
\dot{X}_{i}\dot{A}_{i}-\dot{X}_{i}\dot{X}^{\top}_{i}\gamma_{X,A}\end{pmatrix},

\displaystyle\bar{B}=N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}B_{i}=\begin{pmatrix}S_{ZA,\textup{in}}-S_{XZ,\textup{in}}^{\top}\gamma_{X,A}\\
S_{XA,\textup{in}}-S_{X,\textup{in}}\gamma_{X,A}\end{pmatrix},

(S125)

where

\displaystyle S_{ZA,\textup{in}}=N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\dot{Z}_{i}\dot{A}_{i},

\displaystyle S_{XZ,\textup{in}}=N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\dot{X}_{i}\dot{Z}_{i},

\displaystyle S_{XA,\textup{in}}=N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\dot{X}_{i}\dot{A}_{i},

\displaystyle S_{X,\textup{in}}=N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\dot{X}_{i}\dot{X}^{\top}_{i},\quad\gamma_{X,A}\overset{\eqref{eq:rrifex_def}}{=}\{\E(S_{X,\textup{in}})\}^{-1}\E(S_{XA,\textup{in}}).

Lemma
S12
(
iii
)–(
iv
) ensures
\E(S_{ZA,\textup{in}})=0
and
\E(S_{XZ,\textup{in}})=0
,
so that

\displaystyle\E(\bar{B})\overset{\eqref{eq:bb}}{=}\begin{pmatrix}\E(S_{ZA,\textup{in}})-\E(S_{XZ,\textup{in}}^{\top})\gamma_{X,A}\\
\E(S_{XA,\textup{in}})-\E(S_{X,\textup{in}})\gamma_{X,A}\end{pmatrix}=0_{1+p}.

(S126)

By Lemma
S14
(
iv
), Assumption
S2
(
i
)–(
ii
) and (
iv
) ensure that
\{B_{i}:i\in\mathcal{I}\}
is uniformly integrable, and therefore satisfies the conditions in Lemma
S1
(
ii
).
Applying Lemma
S1
(
ii
) to
B_{i}
ensures

\Omega_{N,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}^{-1/2}\cdot\sqrt{N}\bar{B}\rightsquigarrow\mathcal{N}(0_{1+p},I_{1+p})
.

A technical subtlety is that
\gamma_{X,A}=\{\E(S_{X,\textup{in}})\}^{-1}\E(S_{XA,\textup{in}})
, as defined in (
S20
), may vary with the sample size
N
, so that
B_{i}
is
B_{Ni}
as a triangular array.
While the original theory in
Hansen and
Lee 2019
assumes a sequence setting, we have confirmed with Professor Hansen that the results extend to the asymptotic framework under triangular array settings.

\displaystyle\|B_{i}^{*}\|^{2}=\|\Gamma^{*}_{N}B_{i}\|^{2}\leq\|\Gamma^{*}_{N}\|^{2}\cdot\|B_{i}\|^{2}=\lambda_{0}\|B_{i}\|^{2},

so that

\displaystyle 1_{\{\|B_{i}^{*}\|>M\}}

\displaystyle\E\left(\|B_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\cdot 1_{\{\|B_{i}^{*}\|>M\}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

(S128)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{D}_{[g]}(\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}-\tau_{\textup{c}})-\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{X}_{[g]}(\hat{\beta}_{X,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}-\gamma_{X,A})

\displaystyle-\hat{\gamma}_{X,Z}^{\top}\dot{X}_{[g]}^{\top}R_{{[g]},{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}+\hat{\gamma}_{X,Z}^{\top}\dot{X}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{D}_{[g]}(\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}-\tau_{\textup{c}})+\hat{\gamma}_{X,Z}^{\top}\dot{X}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{X}_{[g]}(\hat{\beta}_{X,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}-\gamma_{X,A}),

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:sxg}}{=}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}-\tau_{\textup{c}})S_{ZD,g}}_{\delta_{g,1}}\underbrace{-(\hat{\beta}_{X,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}-\gamma_{X,A})^{\top}S_{XZ,g}}_{\delta_{g,2}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\hat{\gamma}_{X,Z}^{\top}S_{XR,g}}_{\delta_{g,3}}+\underbrace{\hat{\gamma}_{X,Z}^{\top}S_{XD,g}(\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}-\tau_{\textup{c}})}_{\delta_{g,4}}+\underbrace{\hat{\gamma}_{X,Z}^{\top}S_{X,g}(\hat{\beta}_{X,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}-\gamma_{X,A})}_{\delta_{g,5}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{g,1}+\delta_{g,2}+\delta_{g,3}+\delta_{g,4}+\delta_{g,5},

\displaystyle\delta_{g}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\delta_{g,1}+\delta_{g,2}+\delta_{g,3}+\delta_{g,4}+\delta_{g,5})^{2}\overset{\text{Cauchy--Schwarz}}{\leq}5\left(\sum_{k=1}^{5}\delta_{g,k}^{2}\right),

(S150)

where

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lllll}\delta_{g,1}=-(\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}-\tau_{\textup{c}})S_{ZD,g},&&\delta_{g,2}=-(\hat{\beta}_{X,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}-\gamma_{X,A})^{\top}S_{XZ,g},\\
\delta_{g,3}=-\hat{\gamma}_{X,Z}^{\top}S_{XR,g},&&\delta_{g,4}=(\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}-\tau_{\textup{c}})\hat{\gamma}_{X,Z}^{\top}S_{XD,g},&&\delta_{g,5}=\hat{\gamma}_{X,Z}^{\top}S_{X,g}(\hat{\beta}_{X,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}-\gamma_{X,A}).\end{array}

Equation (
S116
) and the central limit theorem of
\bar{B}
in (
S4.2.1
) we just proved ensure

\displaystyle\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}-\tau_{\textup{c}}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1),\quad\hat{\beta}_{X,{\textup{2sfe}\textup{-x}}}-\gamma_{X,A}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

Combining this with

•

\hat{\gamma}_{X,Z}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
from (
S138
)

•

N^{-1}\displaystyle\sum_{g=1}^{G}S_{ZD,g}^{2}=O(1)
,
N^{-1}\displaystyle\sum_{g=1}^{G}S_{XZ,g}S_{XZ,g}^{\top}=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
,
N^{-1}\displaystyle\sum_{g=1}^{G}S_{XD,g}S_{XD,g}^{\top}=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
,

N^{-1}\displaystyle\sum_{g=1}^{G}\|S_{X,g}\|_{2}^{2}=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
,
N^{-1}\displaystyle\sum_{g=1}^{G}S_{XR,g}S_{XR,g}^{\top}=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)

from Lemma
S13
(
ii
) and Lemma
S14
(
vi
)

ensures that

N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\delta_{g,k}^{2}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)\quad\text{for}\ \ k=1,2,3,4,5.

(S152)

Combining (
S152
) with (
S4.2.2
) implies (

) as follows:

\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\delta_{g}^{2}\overset{\eqref{eq:cs_fex}}{\leq}5N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{k=1}^{5}\delta_{g,k}^{2}=5\sum_{k=1}^{5}\left(N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\delta_{g,k}^{2}\right)\overset{\eqref{eq:dgk}}{=}o_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

V_{\textup{2sfe},N}&=&\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{(\kappa_{N}\cdot\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}})^{2}}\cdot\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\var\left\{\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}(Z_{i}-\bar{Z}_{g})(A_{i}-\bar{A}_{g})\right\},\end{array}

with

N\hat{\textup{se}}_{\textup{2sls}}^{2}-V_{\textup{2sls},N}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1),\quad N\hat{\textup{se}}_{\textup{2sfe}}^{2}-V_{\textup{2sfe},N}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rcl}\displaystyle\sum_{g=1}^{G}\var\left\{\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}(Z_{i}-e)(A_{i}-\mu_{A})\right\}&\overset{\text{Lemma~\ref{lem:efficiency}}}{=}&\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\sum_{g=1}^{G}\left(\sigma^{2}_{\epsilon}\cdot n_{g}+\sigma^{2}_{\alpha}\cdot n_{g}^{2}\phi_{g}\right)\\
&=&\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{Z}(N\sigma^{2}_{\epsilon}+Nc_{N}\sigma^{2}_{\alpha})\\
&=&\displaystyle N\sigma^{2}_{Z}(\sigma^{2}_{\epsilon}+c_{N}\sigma^{2}_{\alpha}),\\
\displaystyle\sum_{g=1}^{G}\var\left\{\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}(Z_{i}-\bar{Z}_{g})(A_{i}-\bar{A}_{g})\right\}&\overset{\text{Lemma~\ref{lem:efficiency}}}{=}&\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{\epsilon}\cdot\sum_{g=1}^{G}\E(S_{Z,g})\\

Plugging (

) into (

Proof of (

By definition,

so that

\dfrac{v_{\textup{2sls},N}}{v_{\textup{2sfe},N}}>1\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad\dfrac{\sigma^{2}_{\alpha}}{\sigma^{2}_{\epsilon}}\cdot c_{N}>\dfrac{1}{\kappa_{N}}-1.

Proof of Corollary

.

When
n_{g}=\bar{n}
, we have

c_{N}=N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}^{2}\phi_{g}=N^{-1}\cdot\bar{n}^{2}\cdot\sum_{g=1}^{G}\phi_{g}=\dfrac{G}{N}\cdot\bar{n}^{2}\cdot\bar{\phi}=\bar{n}\cdot\bar{\phi}

and
\kappa_{N}=1-N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}\phi_{g}=1-N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}N/G\cdot\phi_{g}=1-\bar{\phi}
by Lemma
S11

for

so that
\kappa_{N}=1-G/N=1-\bar{n}^{-1}
and
c_{N}=N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}=1

by Lemma
S11
.

The results then follow from Theorem

\displaystyle R_{{[g]},{\textup{2sls}\textup{-x}}}=(R_{g1,{\textup{2sls}\textup{-x}}},\ldots,R_{g,n_{g},{\textup{2sls}\textup{-x}}})^{\top}=1_{n_{g}}\alpha_{g}^{\prime}+\epsilon_{{[g]}},

S6
Proofs of the results in Section
4

Lemma S17
.

Let
\overline{ZD}=N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}Z_{i}D_{i}
and
\overline{ZY}=N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}Z_{i}Y_{i}
.
As
N\to\infty
, if

(i)

Assumptions
1
–
2
and
4
hold;

(ii)

\displaystyle\max_{g=1,\ldots,G}n_{g}/N\to 0
;

(iii)

\sup_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\E(Y_{i}^{2})<\infty
,

then

\begin{array}[]{lllll}\bar{Z}=\E(\bar{Z})+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1),&&\bar{D}=\E(\bar{D})+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1),&&\bar{Y}=\E(\bar{Y})+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1),\\
\overline{ZD}=\E(\overline{ZD})+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1),&&\overline{ZY}=\E(\overline{ZY})+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1),\\
S_{ZD,\textup{in}}=\E(S_{ZD,\textup{in}})+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1),&&S_{ZY,\textup{in}}=\E(S_{ZY,\textup{in}})+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1).\end{array}

Proof of Lemma
S17
.

Recall that

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lllll}S_{ZD,\textup{in}}&=&\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}(Z_{i}-\bar{Z}_{{c(i)}})(D_{i}-\bar{D}_{{c(i)}})&=&\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\dot{Z}_{i}\dot{D}_{i},\\
\displaystyle S_{ZY,\textup{in}}&=&\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}(Z_{i}-\bar{Z}_{{c(i)}})(Y_{i}-\bar{Y}_{{c(i)}})&=&\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\dot{Z}_{i}\dot{Y}_{i}\end{array}

are the sample means of
\dot{Z}_{i}\dot{D}_{i}
and
\dot{Z}_{i}\dot{Y}_{i}
, respectively.

First,
|Z_{i}|,\,|D_{i}|,\,|Z_{i}D_{i}|,\,|\dot{Z}_{i}\dot{D}_{i}|
are all bounded between
[0,1]
, and therefore uniformly integrable over
i\in\mathcal{I}
.
In addition,
Lemma
S5
(
ii
) ensures

\E(|\dot{Z}_{i}\dot{Y}_{i}|^{2})\leq\E(\dot{Y}_{i}^{2})\leq 2^{2}\cdot\E(Y_{i}^{2}),

so that the condition
\sup_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\E(Y_{i}^{2})<\infty
in Lemma
S17
(
iii
) ensures
that
Z_{i}Y_{i}
,
Y_{i}
, and
\dot{Z}_{i}\dot{Y}_{i}
are all uniformly integrable over
i\in\mathcal{I}
by Lemma
S7
(
iv
).

The results then follow from applying Lemma
S1
(
i
) to

B_{i}=Z_{i},\,D_{i},\,Y_{i},\,Z_{i}D_{i},\,Z_{i}Y_{i},\,\dot{Z}_{i}\dot{D}_{i},\,\dot{Z}_{i}\dot{Y}_{i},

respectively.
∎

Proof of Theorem
2
.

Recall from Lemma
S12
that
\widetilde{Y}_{i}=Y_{i}(1)
for always-takers and
\widetilde{Y}_{i}=Y_{i}(0)
for compliers and never-takers.
Let

denote the common value of

for units within cluster

.
Lemma
S12
ensures that

\displaystyle\E(Y_{{[g]}}\mid Z_{{[g]}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1_{n_{g}}\widetilde{\mu}_{g}+Z_{{[g]}}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c},g}\tau_{\textup{c},g},

(S177)

\displaystyle\E(D_{{[g]}}\mid Z_{{[g]}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1_{n_{g}}\pi_{\textup{a},g}+Z_{{[g]}}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c},g}.

(S178)

We verify below the probability limits for
\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}}
and
\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sls}}
, respectively.

Recall from (

) that
S_{Z,g}=\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}(Z_{i}-\bar{Z}_{g})^{2}=Z_{{[g]}}^{\top}P_{n_{g}}Z_{{[g]}}
.
Let
S_{ZY,g}=\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}(Z_{i}-\bar{Z}_{g})(Y_{i}-\bar{Y}_{g})=Z_{{[g]}}^{\top}P_{n_{g}}Y_{{[g]}}
.
Equation (
S177
) ensures that

\displaystyle\E(S_{ZY,g}\mid Z_{{[g]}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\E(Z_{{[g]}}^{\top}P_{n_{g}}Y_{{[g]}}\mid Z_{{[g]}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Z_{{[g]}}^{\top}P_{n_{g}}\cdot\E(Y_{{[g]}}\mid Z_{{[g]}})

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:hetero_eyz}}{=}

\displaystyle Z_{{[g]}}^{\top}\underbrace{P_{n_{g}}1_{n_{g}}}_{=0}\widetilde{\mu}_{g}+Z_{{[g]}}^{\top}P_{n_{g}}Z_{{[g]}}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c},g}\tau_{\textup{c},g}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\pi_{\textup{c},g}\tau_{\textup{c},g}\cdot S_{Z,g},

so that

\displaystyle\E(S_{ZY,g})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\E\Big\{\E(S_{ZY,g}\mid Z_{{[g]}})\Big\}=\pi_{\textup{c},g}\tau_{\textup{c},g}\cdot\E(S_{Z,g}),

\displaystyle\E(S_{ZY,\textup{in}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\E\left(S_{ZY,g}\right)=N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\pi_{\textup{c},g}\tau_{\textup{c},g}\cdot\E(S_{Z,g}).

(S179)

Identical reasoning ensures

\displaystyle\E(S_{ZD,\textup{in}})=N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\pi_{\textup{c},g}\cdot\E(S_{Z,g}).

(S180)

given (
S178
).
It then follows from (
S31
) and Lemma
S17
that

\displaystyle\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:num}}{=}

\displaystyle\dfrac{S_{ZY,\textup{in}}}{S_{ZD,\textup{in}}}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{Lemma~\ref{lem:hetero}}}{=}

\displaystyle\dfrac{\E(S_{ZY,\textup{in}})}{\E(S_{ZD,\textup{in}})}+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:hetero_zpiny}+\eqref{eq:hetero_zpind}}{=}

\displaystyle\dfrac{\sum_{g=1}^{G}\pi_{\textup{c},g}\tau_{\textup{c},g}\cdot\E(S_{Z,g})}{\sum_{g=1}^{G}\pi_{\textup{c},g}\cdot\E(S_{Z,g})}+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{g=1}^{G}\kappa_{g,\textup{2sfe}}\cdot\tau_{\textup{c},g}+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1),

where

\kappa_{g,\textup{2sfe}}=\dfrac{\pi_{\textup{c},g}\cdot\E(S_{Z,g})}{\sum_{g=1}^{G}\pi_{\textup{c},g}\cdot\E(S_{Z,g})}.

In addition, the expression of
\E(S_{Z,g})
in Lemma
S11
implies that

\displaystyle\E(S_{Z,g})\overset{\textup{Lemma~\ref{lem:Z}}}{=}\sigma_{Z,g}^{2}\cdot n_{g}(1-\phi_{g})

(S181)

under Assumptions
1
–
2
and
4
, so that the expression of
\kappa_{g,\textup{2sfe}}
in (
S6
) equals

\displaystyle\kappa_{g,\textup{2sfe}}\overset{\eqref{eq:kgfe_def}+\eqref{eq:eszg_hetero}}{=}\dfrac{n_{g}(1-\phi_{g})\sigma_{Z,g}^{2}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c},g}}{\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}(1-\phi_{g})\sigma_{Z,g}^{2}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c},g}}.

Probability limit of
\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sls}}
.

Recall from (
S31
) that

\displaystyle\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sls}}=\dfrac{S_{ZY}}{S_{ZD}},

(S182)

where
S_{ZY}=N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}(Z_{i}-\bar{Z})(Y_{i}-\bar{Y})
and
S_{ZD}=N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}(Z_{i}-\bar{Z})(D_{i}-\bar{D})
. We compute below the probability limits of
S_{ZY}
and
S_{ZD}
, respectively, which together imply the results.

Lemma
S12
(
i
)–(
ii
) imply that

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{llllll}\E(Y_{i})=\widetilde{\mu}_{g}+e_{g}\pi_{\textup{c},g}\tau_{\textup{c},g},&&\E(Z_{i}Y_{i})=e_{g}(\widetilde{\mu}_{g}+\pi_{\textup{c},g}\tau_{\textup{c},g}),&&\cov(Z_{i},Y_{i})=\sigma_{Z,g}^{2}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c},g}\tau_{\textup{c},g},\\
\E(D_{i})=\pi_{\textup{a},g}+e_{g}\pi_{\textup{c},g},&&\E(Z_{i}D_{i})=e_{g}(\pi_{\textup{a},g}+\pi_{\textup{c},g}),&&\cov(Z_{i},D_{i})=\sigma_{Z,g}^{2}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c},g}\end{array}\qquad

for
{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}
under Assumptions
1
–
2
and
4
.
This ensures

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rcl}\E(\bar{Z})&=&\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}\E(Z_{i})=N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}e_{g},\\
\E(\bar{D})&=&\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}\E(D_{i})\overset{\eqref{eq:ey_ed_hetero}}{=}N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}(\pi_{\textup{a},g}+e_{g}\pi_{\textup{c},g}),\\
\E\left(\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}Z_{i}D_{i}\right)&=&\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}\E(Z_{i}D_{i})\overset{\eqref{eq:ey_ed_hetero}}{=}N^{-1}\displaystyle\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}e_{g}(\pi_{\textup{a},g}+\pi_{\textup{c},g}),\end{array}

so that

\displaystyle S_{ZD}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}(Z_{i}-\bar{Z})(D_{i}-\bar{D})

(S190)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}Z_{i}D_{i}-\bar{Z}\bar{D}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{Lemma~\ref{lem:hetero}}}{=}

\displaystyle\E\left(N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}Z_{i}D_{i}\right)-\E(\bar{Z})\cdot\E(\bar{D})+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:hetero_zpd_0}}{=}

\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}e_{g}(\pi_{\textup{a},g}+\pi_{\textup{c},g})

\displaystyle-\left(N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}e_{g}\right)\left\{N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}(\pi_{\textup{a},g}+e_{g}\pi_{\textup{c},g})\right\}+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1).\qquad

Identical reasoning ensures that

\displaystyle S_{ZY}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}(Z_{i}-\bar{Z})(Y_{i}-\bar{Y})

(S191)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}e_{g}(\widetilde{\mu}_{g}+\pi_{\textup{c},g}\tau_{\textup{c},g})

\displaystyle-\left(N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}e_{g}\right)\left\{N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}(\widetilde{\mu}_{g}+e_{g}\pi_{\textup{c},g}\tau_{\textup{c},g})\right\}+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

Plugging (
S190
)–(
S191
) in (
S182
) verifies the result.
∎

Let

from (

(S221)

Combining (

Write

We verify in Section

(S223)

and

S6.1.2.1
Proof of

in (

).

From (
S219
), let

\check{Z}_{[g]}=(Z_{i}-e:{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}})=Z_{{[g]}}-1_{n_{g}}e,\qquad\check{A}_{[g]}=(A_{i}-\mu_{A}:{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}})=\alpha_{g}-1_{n_{g}}\mu_{A}

to write

\widetilde{B}_{g}^{*}=\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}\dfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}}}\begin{pmatrix}(Z_{i}-e)(A_{i}-\mu_{A})\\
\kappa_{N}^{-1}\dot{Z}_{i}\dot{A}_{i}\end{pmatrix}=\dfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}}}\begin{pmatrix}\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}\\
\kappa_{N}^{-1}\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}}\end{pmatrix}.

(S224)

Plugging (
S224
) into the definition of
\widetilde{V}_{N}
in (
S219
) ensures that

\displaystyle\widetilde{V}_{N}[1,2]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:goal_joint_hse}}{=}

\displaystyle(1,0)\widetilde{V}_{N}\begin{pmatrix}0\\
1\end{pmatrix}

(S225)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:tvn_def}}{=}

\displaystyle(1,0)\left(N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\widetilde{B}_{g}^{*}\widetilde{B}_{g}^{*\top}\right)\begin{pmatrix}0\\
1\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:tbg*}}{=}

\displaystyle(1,0)\cdot\left\{\dfrac{1}{(\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}})^{2}}\cdot\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\begin{pmatrix}\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}\\
\kappa_{N}^{-1}\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}}\end{pmatrix}(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]},\kappa_{N}^{-1}\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}})\right\}\cdot\begin{pmatrix}0\\
1\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{(\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}})^{2}}\cdot\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\underbrace{(1,0)\begin{pmatrix}\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}\\
\kappa_{N}^{-1}\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}}\end{pmatrix}}_{=\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}}\underbrace{(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]},\kappa_{N}^{-1}\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}})\begin{pmatrix}0\\
1\end{pmatrix}}_{=\kappa_{N}^{-1}\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{(\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}})^{2}}\cdot\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\left(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}\right)\left(\kappa_{N}^{-1}\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}}}\cdot\dfrac{1}{\kappa_{N}\cdot\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}}}\cdot\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\left(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}\right)\left(\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}}}\cdot\dfrac{1}{\kappa_{N}\cdot\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}}}\cdot\widetilde{T},

where

\widetilde{T}=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\widetilde{T}_{g},\qquad\widetilde{T}_{g}=\left(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}\right)\left(\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}}\right).

On the other hand,

\displaystyle N\hat{\sigma}_{\textup{2sls},\textup{2sfe}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{S_{ZD}}\cdot\dfrac{1}{S_{ZD,\textup{in}}}\cdot\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\left\{\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}(Z_{i}-\bar{Z})r_{i,\textup{2sls}}\right\}\left(\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}\dot{Z}_{i}r_{i,\textup{2sfe}}\right)

(S226)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{S_{ZD}}\cdot\dfrac{1}{S_{ZD,\textup{in}}}\cdot\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\left\{(Z_{{[g]}}-1_{n_{g}}\bar{Z})^{\top}r_{{[g]},\textup{2sls}}\right\}\left(\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}r_{{[g]},\textup{2sfe}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{S_{ZD}}\cdot\dfrac{1}{S_{ZD,\textup{in}}}\cdot\widehat{T},

where

\widehat{T}=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\widehat{T}_{g},\qquad\widehat{T}_{g}=\left\{(Z_{{[g]}}-1_{n_{g}}\bar{Z})^{\top}r_{{[g]},\textup{2sls}}\right\}\left(\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}r_{{[g]},\textup{2sfe}}\right),

and

S_{ZD}=\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}}+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1),\quad S_{ZD,\textup{in}}=\kappa_{N}\cdot\sigma^{2}_{Z}\cdot\pi_{\textup{c}}+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)

(S227)

by Lemma
S13
(
ii
).
Given (
S225
)–(
S227
), it suffices to verify that

\displaystyle\widehat{T}-\widetilde{T}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

(S228)

Proof of
\widehat{T}-\widetilde{T}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
in (
S228
).

It follows from

r_{i,\textup{2sls}}=Y_{i}-\hat{\beta}_{1}-D_{i}\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sls}},\quad A_{i}=Y_{i}-D_{i}\tau_{\textup{c}};\qquad r_{i,\textup{2sfe}}=\dot{Y}_{i}-\dot{D}_{i}\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}},\quad\dot{A}_{i}=\dot{Y}_{i}-\dot{D}_{i}\tau_{\textup{c}}

that

r_{i,\textup{2sls}}-(A_{i}-\mu_{A})=-(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\mu_{A})-D_{i}(\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sls}}-\tau_{\textup{c}}),\quad r_{i,\textup{2sfe}}-\dot{A}_{i}=-\dot{D}_{i}(\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}}-\tau_{\textup{c}}),

\begin{array}[]{rcl}r_{{[g]},\textup{2sls}}-\check{A}_{[g]}&=&-1_{n_{g}}(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\mu_{A})-D_{{[g]}}(\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sls}}-\tau_{\textup{c}})\\
&=&-(1_{n_{g}},D_{{[g]}})\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\beta}_{1}-\mu_{A}\\
\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sls}}-\tau_{\textup{c}}\end{pmatrix}\\
&=&-(1_{n_{g}},D_{{[g]}})\delta_{\textup{2sls}},\\
r_{{[g]},\textup{2sfe}}-\dot{A}_{{[g]}}&=&-\dot{D}_{[g]}(\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}}-\tau_{\textup{c}})=-\dot{D}_{[g]}\delta_{\textup{2sfe}},\end{array}

(S229)

where

\displaystyle\delta_{\textup{2sls}}=\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\beta}_{1}-\mu_{A}\\
\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sls}}-\tau_{\textup{c}}\end{pmatrix},\qquad\delta_{\textup{2sfe}}=\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}}-\tau_{\textup{c}}.

Combining (
S229
) with

Z_{{[g]}}-1_{n_{g}}\bar{Z}=Z_{{[g]}}-1_{n_{g}}e-1_{n_{g}}(\bar{Z}-e)=\check{Z}_{[g]}-1_{n_{g}}(\bar{Z}-e)

(S230)

yields

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rcl}(Z_{{[g]}}-1_{n_{g}}\bar{Z})^{\top}r_{{[g]},\textup{2sls}}&\overset{\eqref{eq:zg}+\eqref{eq:rgols_rgfe}}{=}&\left\{\check{Z}_{[g]}-1_{n_{g}}(\bar{Z}-e)\right\}^{\top}\left\{\check{A}_{[g]}-(1_{n_{g}},D_{{[g]}})\delta_{\textup{2sls}}\right\}\\
&=&\left\{\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}-(\bar{Z}-e)1_{n_{g}}^{\top}\right\}\left\{\check{A}_{[g]}-(1_{n_{g}},D_{{[g]}})\delta_{\textup{2sls}}\right\}\\
&=&\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}-\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}(1_{n_{g}},D_{{[g]}})\delta_{\textup{2sls}}\\
&&-(\bar{Z}-e)1_{n_{g}}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}+(\bar{Z}-e)1_{n_{g}}^{\top}(1_{n_{g}},D_{{[g]}})\delta_{\textup{2sls}}\\
&=&\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}+\xi_{g,\textup{2sls}},\\
\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}r_{{[g]},\textup{2sfe}}&\overset{\eqref{eq:rgols_rgfe}}{=}&\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}(\dot{A}_{{[g]}}-\dot{D}_{[g]}\delta_{\textup{2sfe}})=\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}}+\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}},\end{array}

where

\displaystyle\xi_{g,\textup{2sls}}=-\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}(1_{n_{g}},D_{{[g]}})\delta_{\textup{2sls}}-(\bar{Z}-e)1_{n_{g}}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}+(\bar{Z}-e)1_{n_{g}}^{\top}(1_{n_{g}},D_{{[g]}})\delta_{\textup{2sls}},\qquad\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}}=-\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{D}_{[g]}\delta_{\textup{2sfe}}.

Plugging (
S6.1.2
) into the definition of
(\widehat{T},\widehat{T}_{g})
in (
S226
) ensures

\displaystyle\widehat{T}_{g}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:hclsfe_app}}{=}

\displaystyle\left\{(Z_{{[g]}}-1_{n_{g}}\bar{Z})^{\top}r_{{[g]},\textup{2sls}}\right\}\left(\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}r_{{[g]},\textup{2sfe}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}+\xi_{g,\textup{2sls}}\right)\left(\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}}+\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}\right)\left(\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}}\right)+\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}}\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}+\xi_{g,\textup{2sls}}\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}}+\xi_{g,\textup{2sls}}\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}},

\displaystyle\widehat{T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\widetilde{T}_{g}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\left(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}\right)\left(\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}}\right)+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}}\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\xi_{g,\textup{2sls}}\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}}+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\xi_{g,\textup{2sls}}\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widetilde{T}+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}}\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\xi_{g,\textup{2sls}}\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}}+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\xi_{g,\textup{2sls}}\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}}.

so that

\displaystyle\widehat{T}-\widetilde{T}=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}}\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\xi_{g,\textup{2sls}}\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}}+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\xi_{g,\textup{2sls}}\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}}.

(S238)

From (
S238
), a sufficient condition for
\widehat{T}-\widetilde{T}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
in (
S228
) to hold is that

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}}\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1),\quad\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\xi_{g,\textup{2sls}}\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{A}_{{[g]}}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1),\quad\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\xi_{g,\textup{2sls}}\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1).\qquad

(S239)

We provide below the proof of the first equality,
N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}}\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
, in (
S239
). The proofs of the remaining two equalities are analogous and therefore omitted.

Proof of
N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}}\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
in (
S239
).

Recall from (
S6.1.2
) that
\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}}=-\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{D}_{[g]}\delta_{\textup{2sfe}}
with
\delta_{\textup{2sfe}}=\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}}-\tau_{\textup{c}}
from (
S229
) to write

N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}}\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}=-\delta_{\textup{2sfe}}\cdot N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\left(\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{D}_{[g]}\right)\left(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}\right).

(S240)

The central limit theorem for
(\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sls}}-\tau_{\textup{c}},\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}}-\tau_{\textup{c}})^{\top}
in (
S192
) that we just verified implies

\delta_{\textup{2sfe}}=\hat{\tau}_{\textup{2sfe}}-\tau_{\textup{c}}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

(S241)

We show below that

T=N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\left(\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{D}_{[g]}\right)\left(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}\right)=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

(S242)

Combining (
S240
)–(
S242
) implies
N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\xi_{g,\textup{2sfe}}\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
.

Proof of (
S242
).

It follows from
(\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{D}_{[g]})(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]})\leq 2^{-1}\{(\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{D}_{[g]})^{2}+(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]})^{2}\}
that

\displaystyle T

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\left(\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{D}_{[g]}\right)\left(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}\right)

(S243)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle N^{-1}\cdot 2^{-1}\left\{\sum_{g=1}^{G}\left(\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{D}_{[g]}\right)^{2}+\sum_{g=1}^{G}\left(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}\right)^{2}\right\},

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{-1}(T_{1}+T_{2}),

where

\displaystyle T_{1}=N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\left(\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{D}_{[g]}\right)^{2},\quad T_{2}=N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\left(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}\right)^{2}.

First, it follows from
\dot{Z}_{i},\dot{D}_{i}\in(-1,1)
that

\left|\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{D}_{[g]}\right|=\left|\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}\dot{Z}_{i}\dot{D}_{i}\right|\leq n_{g}
,
so that

T_{1}=N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\left(\dot{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\dot{D}_{[g]}\right)^{2}\leq N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}^{2}=O(1)

(S244)

under Assumption
5
.

Similarly, it follows from
|Z_{i}-e|<1
that

\left(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}\right)^{2}=\left|\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}(Z_{i}-e)(A_{i}-\mu_{A})\right|^{2}<\left(\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}|A_{i}-\mu_{A}|\right)^{2}\leq n_{g}\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}(A_{i}-\mu_{A})^{2}

by Cauchy–Schwarz inequality,
so that

T_{2}=N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\left(\check{Z}_{[g]}^{\top}\check{A}_{[g]}\right)^{2}\leq N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}\left\{\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}(A_{i}-\mu_{A})^{2}\right\}.

\E(|T_{2}|)\leq N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}\left[\sum_{{i\in\mathcal{I}_{g}}}\E\left\{(A_{i}-\mu_{A})^{2}\right\}\right]=\var(A_{i})\cdot N^{-1}\sum_{g=1}^{G}n_{g}^{2}=O(1)

under Assumption
5
. This implies

T_{2}=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)

(S245)

by Lemma
S6
(
iib
).
Plugging (
S244
)–(
S245
) into (
S243
) ensures
T=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
in (
S242
).
